/* ============================================================ M02: УТИЛИТЫ Только var и function. Без let/const/arrow/async/await. Все публичные функции экспортируются в window. ============================================================ */ (function () { 'use strict'; /* ---------- ESCAPING ---------- */ function esc(s) { if (s === null || s === undefined) return ''; return String(s) .replace(/&/g, '&') .replace(//g, '>') .replace(/"/g, '"') .replace(/'/g, '''); } /* ---------- ФОРМАТИРОВАНИЕ ЧИСЕЛ ---------- */ function formatMoney(n) { var v = Number(n); if (!isFinite(v)) v = 0; var rounded = Math.round(v); var parts = String(Math.abs(rounded)).split(''); var out = ''; var cnt = 0; for (var i = parts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { out = parts[i] + out; cnt++; if (cnt % 3 === 0 && i > 0) out = ' ' + out; } return (rounded < 0 ? '−' : '') + out + ' ₽'; } function formatMoney2(n) { var v = Number(n); if (!isFinite(v)) v = 0; var neg = v < 0; v = Math.abs(v); var rub = Math.floor(v); var kop = Math.round((v - rub) * 100); if (kop === 100) { rub++; kop = 0; } var kopStr = kop < 10 ? '0' + kop : String(kop); var rubStr = formatMoney(rub).replace(' ₽', ''); return (neg ? '−' : '') + rubStr + ',' + kopStr + ' ₽'; } function formatMillions(n) { var v = Number(n); if (!isFinite(v)) return '0 млн ₽'; if (Math.abs(v) >= 1000000) { return (v / 1000000).toFixed(2).replace('.', ',').replace(/,00$/, '') + ' млн ₽'; } if (Math.abs(v) >= 1000) { return (v / 1000).toFixed(0) + ' тыс ₽'; } return formatMoney(v); } function formatPercent(n, digits) { var v = Number(n); if (!isFinite(v)) v = 0; var d = (digits === undefined || digits === null) ? 1 : digits; return v.toFixed(d).replace('.', ',') + '%'; } function formatNumber(n) { var v = Number(n); if (!isFinite(v)) return '0'; var neg = v < 0; v = Math.abs(v); var s = String(Math.round(v * 100) / 100); var parts = s.split('.'); var intPart = parts[0]; var fracPart = parts[1] || ''; var out = ''; var cnt = 0; for (var i = intPart.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { out = intPart.charAt(i) + out; cnt++; if (cnt % 3 === 0 && i > 0) out = ' ' + out; } if (fracPart) out = out + ',' + fracPart; return (neg ? '−' : '') + out; } /* ---------- ДАТЫ ---------- */ function todayISO() { var d = new Date(); return dateToISO(d); } function dateToISO(d) { if (!(d instanceof Date) || isNaN(d.getTime())) return ''; var y = d.getFullYear(); var m = d.getMonth() + 1; var day = d.getDate(); return String(y) + '-' + (m < 10 ? '0' + m : m) + '-' + (day < 10 ? '0' + day : day); } var MONTHS_RU = ['января','февраля','марта','апреля','мая','июня','июля','августа','сентября','октября','ноября','декабря']; var MONTHS_RU_NOM = ['Январь','Февраль','Март','Апрель','Май','Июнь','Июль','Август','Сентябрь','Октябрь','Ноябрь','Декабрь']; var MONTHS_RU_GEN = ['января','февраля','марта','апреля','мая','июня','июля','августа','сентября','октября','ноября','декабря']; var MONTHS_EN = ['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec']; function parseISO(iso) { if (!iso) return null; var parts = String(iso).split('-'); if (parts.length !== 3) return null; var y = parseInt(parts[0], 10); var m = parseInt(parts[1], 10) - 1; var d = parseInt(parts[2], 10); if (isNaN(y) || isNaN(m) || isNaN(d)) return null; return new Date(y, m, d); } function formatDateRu(iso) { var d = parseISO(iso); if (!d) return ''; var day = d.getDate(); var m = d.getMonth(); return day + ' ' + MONTHS_RU_GEN[m] + ' ' + d.getFullYear(); } function formatDateRuLong(iso) { var d = parseISO(iso); if (!d) return ''; var day = d.getDate(); var m = d.getMonth(); return day + ' ' + MONTHS_RU[m] + ' ' + d.getFullYear() + ' г.'; } function formatMonthRu(iso) { var d = parseISO(iso + '-01') || parseISO(iso); if (!d) return ''; return MONTHS_RU_NOM[d.getMonth()] + ' ' + d.getFullYear(); } function formatMonthRuGenitive(iso) { var d = parseISO(iso + '-01') || parseISO(iso); if (!d) return ''; return MONTHS_RU_GEN[d.getMonth()] + ' ' + d.getFullYear(); } /* ---------- ФАЙЛЫ ---------- */ function readFileAsDataURL(file, cb) { if (!file || !(file instanceof Blob)) { if (cb) cb(null); return; } var reader = new FileReader(); reader.onload = function (e) { if (cb) cb(e.target.result); }; reader.onerror = function () { if (cb) cb(null); }; reader.readAsDataURL(file); } function readFileAsText(file, cb) { if (!file || !(file instanceof Blob)) { if (cb) cb(null); return; } var reader = new FileReader(); reader.onload = function (e) { if (cb) cb(e.target.result); }; reader.onerror = function () { if (cb) cb(null); }; reader.readAsText(file); } function downloadFile(content, filename, mime) { var blob = new Blob([content], { type: mime || 'application/octet-stream' }); var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob); var a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = filename; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a); setTimeout(function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); }, 1000); } function downloadJSON(obj, filename) { var json = JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2); downloadFile(json, filename || 'backup.json', 'application/json'); } function downloadCSV(rows, filename) { // rows — массив массивов; экранируем var lines = []; for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { var row = rows[i]; var cells = []; for (var j = 0; j < row.length; j++) { var c = row[j] === null || row[j] === undefined ? '' : String(row[j]); if (c.indexOf('"') >= 0 || c.indexOf(',') >= 0 || c.indexOf('\n') >= 0 || c.indexOf(';') >= 0) { c = '"' + c.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"'; } cells.push(c); } lines.push(cells.join(';')); } var csv = '\uFEFF' + lines.join('\r\n'); downloadFile(csv, filename || 'data.csv', 'text/csv;charset=utf-8'); } /* ---------- ХЭШ / КОДЫ ---------- */ function simpleHash(str) { var h = 0x811c9dc5; var s = String(str); for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) { h = h ^ s.charCodeAt(i); h = (h * 0x01000193) >>> 0; } return ('00000000' + h.toString(16)).slice(-8); } function generatePromoCode(prefix) { var chars = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789'; var out = prefix ? String(prefix).toUpperCase() : 'TAX'; for (var i = 0; i < 8; i++) { out += chars.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)); } return out; } /* ---------- АНИМАЦИЯ ---------- */ function animateCounter(el, from, to, duration) { if (!el) return; var start = Number(from) || 0; var end = Number(to) || 0; var dur = duration || 600; var t0 = null; function step(ts) { if (!t0) t0 = ts; var p = Math.min(1, (ts - t0) / dur); var v = start + (end - start) * p; el.textContent = formatMoney(v); if (p < 1) { requestAnimationFrame(step); } else { el.textContent = formatMoney(end); } } requestAnimationFrame(step); } /* ---------- TOAST ---------- */ var toastTimers = {}; function showToast(msg, type, ms) { var container = document.getElementById('toastContainer'); if (!container) return; var t = document.createElement('div'); t.className = 'toast' + (type ? ' ' + type : ''); t.textContent = msg; container.appendChild(t); var life = ms || 2800; setTimeout(function () { if (t.parentNode) t.parentNode.removeChild(t); }, life); } /* ---------- PDF (рендер HTML в PDF через print-окно) ---------- */ function renderHtmlToPdf(html, title) { // Открываем новое окно и печатаем (fallback без jsPDF, если M28 не загружен) if (window.jspdf && window.jspdf.jsPDF) { // Простой вариант: конвертируем текст в PDF (без сложной верстки) try { var doc = new window.jspdf.jsPDF(); doc.setFontSize(12); var plain = String(html).replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').replace(/ /g, ' '); var lines = doc.splitTextToSize(plain, 180); var y = 15; for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) { if (y > 285) { doc.addPage(); y = 15; } doc.text(String(lines[i]), 12, y); y += 6; } doc.save((title || 'document') + '.pdf'); return; } catch (e) { /* fallback ниже */ } } var w = window.open('', '_blank'); if (!w) { showToast('Не удалось открыть окно печати', 'error'); return; } w.document.open(); w.document.write('' + esc(title || 'PDF') + ''); w.document.write(''); w.document.write('' + html + ''); w.document.close(); setTimeout(function () { w.focus(); w.print(); }, 300); } /* ---------- ПРОЧЕЕ ---------- */ function debounce(fn, wait) { var timer = null; return function () { var ctx = this; var args = arguments; if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(function () { fn.apply(ctx, args); }, wait || 250); }; } function clamp(v, min, max) { var n = Number(v); if (!isFinite(n)) n = min; if (n < min) return min; if (n > max) return max; return n; } function num(v, def) { var n = Number(v); if (!isFinite(n)) return def === undefined ? 0 : def; return n; } function int(v, def) { var n = parseInt(v, 10); if (isNaN(n)) return def === undefined ? 0 : def; return n; } function deepClone(obj) { if (obj === null || typeof obj !== 'object') return obj; return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(obj)); } /* ---------- CRYPTO (AES-GCM через Web Crypto) ---------- */ var CryptoUtils = { // Производный ключ из пароля (PBKDF2 -> AES-GCM) deriveKey: function (password, saltB64, cb) { try { var enc = new TextEncoder(); var salt = CryptoUtils._b64ToBytes(saltB64); var subtle = window.crypto && window.crypto.subtle; if (!subtle) { cb(null); return; } subtle.importKey('raw', enc.encode(String(password)), { name: 'PBKDF2' }, false, ['deriveKey']) .then(function (baseKey) { return subtle.deriveKey( { name: 'PBKDF2', salt: salt, iterations: 100000, hash: 'SHA-256' }, baseKey, { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 }, false, ['encrypt', 'decrypt'] ); }) .then(function (key) { cb(key); }) .catch(function () { cb(null); }); } catch (e) { cb(null); } }, encrypt: function (plainText, password, cb) { try { var saltB64 = CryptoUtils._randomB64(16); var ivB64 = CryptoUtils._randomB64(12); CryptoUtils.deriveKey(password, saltB64, function (key) { if (!key) { cb(null); return; } var enc = new TextEncoder(); var subtle = window.crypto.subtle; subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv: CryptoUtils._b64ToBytes(ivB64) }, key, enc.encode(plainText)) .then(function (buf) { var ct = CryptoUtils._bytesToB64(new Uint8Array(buf)); cb({ salt: saltB64, iv: ivB64, data: ct }); }) .catch(function () { cb(null); }); }); } catch (e) { cb(null); } }, decrypt: function (payload, password, cb) { try { if (!payload || !payload.salt || !payload.iv || !payload.data) { cb(null); return; } CryptoUtils.deriveKey(password, payload.salt, function (key) { if (!key) { cb(null); return; } var subtle = window.crypto.subtle; subtle.decrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv: CryptoUtils._b64ToBytes(payload.iv) }, key, CryptoUtils._b64ToBytes(payload.data)) .then(function (buf) { var dec = new TextDecoder(); cb(dec.decode(buf)); }) .catch(function () { cb(null); }); }); } catch (e) { cb(null); } }, _randomB64: function (n) { var arr = new Uint8Array(n); if (window.crypto && window.crypto.getRandomValues) { window.crypto.getRandomValues(arr); } else { for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) arr[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256); } return CryptoUtils._bytesToB64(arr); }, _bytesToB64: function (bytes) { var bin = ''; for (var i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) bin += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]); return btoa(bin); }, _b64ToBytes: function (b64) { var bin = atob(b64); var arr = new Uint8Array(bin.length); for (var i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) arr[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i); return arr; } }; /* ---------- ЭКСПОРТ В WINDOW ---------- */ window.esc = esc; window.formatMoney = formatMoney; window.formatMoney2 = formatMoney2; window.formatMillions = formatMillions; window.formatPercent = formatPercent; window.formatNumber = formatNumber; window.todayISO = todayISO; window.dateToISO = dateToISO; window.parseISO = parseISO; window.formatDateRu = formatDateRu; window.formatDateRuLong = formatDateRuLong; window.formatMonthRu = formatMonthRu; window.formatMonthRuGenitive = formatMonthRuGenitive; window.readFileAsDataURL = readFileAsDataURL; window.readFileAsText = readFileAsText; window.downloadFile = downloadFile; window.downloadJSON = downloadJSON; window.downloadCSV = downloadCSV; window.simpleHash = simpleHash; window.generatePromoCode = generatePromoCode; window.animateCounter = animateCounter; window.showToast = showToast; window.renderHtmlToPdf = renderHtmlToPdf; window.debounce = debounce; window.clamp = clamp; window.num = num; window.int = int; window.deepClone = deepClone; window.CryptoUtils = CryptoUtils; window.MONTHS_RU = MONTHS_RU; window.MONTHS_RU_NOM = MONTHS_RU_NOM; window.MONTHS_RU_GEN = MONTHS_RU_GEN; window.MONTHS_EN = MONTHS_EN; })();/* ============================================================ M03: STORAGE + SANDBOX localStorage с fallback в память, переключение песочницы, бэкап. Только var и function. ============================================================ */ (function () { 'use strict'; /* ---------- ПРЕФИКСЫ ---------- */ var PREFIX = 'taxcalc_'; var SANDBOX_PREFIX = 'taxcalc_sbx_'; /* ---------- ПАМЯТЬ (fallback + песочница) ---------- */ var memoryStore = {}; var sandboxStore = {}; var sandboxActive = false; var lsAvailable = false; /* Проверка доступности localStorage */ function checkLocalStorage() { try { var k = '__tc_test__'; window.localStorage.setItem(k, '1'); window.localStorage.removeItem(k); lsAvailable = true; } catch (e) { lsAvailable = false; } return lsAvailable; } /* ---------- БАЗОВЫЕ ОПЕРАЦИИ ---------- */ function storageKey(key) { if (sandboxActive) return SANDBOX_PREFIX + key; return PREFIX + key; } function getRaw(key) { if (sandboxActive) { if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(sandboxStore, key)) { return sandboxStore[key]; } return null; } if (lsAvailable) { try { return window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey(key)); } catch (e) { return null; } } if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(memoryStore, key)) { return memoryStore[key]; } return null; } function setRaw(key, value) { if (sandboxActive) { sandboxStore[key] = value; return true; } if (lsAvailable) { try { window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey(key), value); return true; } catch (e) { // Переполнение квоты — пробуем в память memoryStore[key] = value; return false; } } memoryStore[key] = value; return true; } function removeRaw(key) { if (sandboxActive) { delete sandboxStore[key]; return; } if (lsAvailable) { try { window.localStorage.removeItem(storageKey(key)); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } return; } delete memoryStore[key]; } function clearRaw() { if (sandboxActive) { sandboxStore = {}; return; } if (lsAvailable) { try { var keys = []; for (var i = 0; i < window.localStorage.length; i++) { var k = window.localStorage.key(i); if (k && k.indexOf(PREFIX) === 0) keys.push(k); } for (var j = 0; j < keys.length; j++) { window.localStorage.removeItem(keys[j]); } } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } } memoryStore = {}; } /* ---------- JSON-API ---------- */ function get(key, def) { var raw = getRaw(key); if (raw === null || raw === undefined) return def === undefined ? null : def; try { return JSON.parse(raw); } catch (e) { return def === undefined ? null : def; } } function set(key, value) { try { var str = JSON.stringify(value); return setRaw(key, str); } catch (e) { return false; } } function remove(key) { removeRaw(key); } function clear() { clearRaw(); } function has(key) { return getRaw(key) !== null; } function keys() { var out = []; if (sandboxActive) { for (var k in sandboxStore) { if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(sandboxStore, k)) out.push(k); } return out; } if (lsAvailable) { try { for (var i = 0; i < window.localStorage.length; i++) { var key = window.localStorage.key(i); if (key && key.indexOf(PREFIX) === 0) { out.push(key.substring(PREFIX.length)); } } } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } return out; } for (var k2 in memoryStore) { if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(memoryStore, k2)) out.push(k2); } return out; } /* ---------- ПЕСОЧНИЦА ---------- */ function isSandbox() { return sandboxActive; } function enableSandbox() { if (sandboxActive) return; sandboxStore = {}; sandboxActive = true; try { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('sandboxchange', { detail: { active: true } })); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } } function disableSandbox(keepData) { if (!sandboxActive) return; if (keepData) { // Переносим данные песочницы в основное хранилище for (var k in sandboxStore) { if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(sandboxStore, k)) { var prevSandbox = sandboxActive; sandboxActive = false; setRaw(k, sandboxStore[k]); sandboxActive = prevSandbox; } } } sandboxStore = {}; sandboxActive = false; try { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('sandboxchange', { detail: { active: false } })); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } } function getSandboxData() { return deepCloneSafe(sandboxStore); } function clearSandbox() { sandboxStore = {}; } /* ---------- БЭКАП ---------- */ function exportAll() { var result = {}; var ks = keys(); for (var i = 0; i < ks.length; i++) { result[ks[i]] = get(ks[i], null); } return { version: 1, exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), data: result }; } function importAll(payload, overwrite) { if (!payload || !payload.data) return false; var ks = Object.keys(payload.data); if (overwrite) clear(); for (var i = 0; i < ks.length; i++) { set(ks[i], payload.data[ks[i]]); } return true; } /* ---------- СТАТИСТИКА ---------- */ function stats() { var out = { keys: 0, bytes: 0, lsAvailable: lsAvailable, sandbox: sandboxActive }; var ks = keys(); out.keys = ks.length; for (var i = 0; i < ks.length; i++) { var raw = getRaw(ks[i]); if (raw) out.bytes += raw.length; } return out; } /* ---------- ВСПОМОГАТЕЛЬНОЕ ---------- */ function deepCloneSafe(obj) { try { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(obj)); } catch (e) { return {}; } } /* ---------- ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ ---------- */ checkLocalStorage(); /* ---------- ЭКСПОРТ В WINDOW ---------- */ window.Storage = { get: get, set: set, remove: remove, clear: clear, has: has, keys: keys, isSandbox: isSandbox, enableSandbox: enableSandbox, disableSandbox: disableSandbox, getSandboxData: getSandboxData, clearSandbox: clearSandbox, exportAll: exportAll, importAll: importAll, stats: stats, isLocalStorageAvailable: function () { return lsAvailable; } }; // Удобные алиасы window.storageGet = get; window.storageSet = set; window.storageRemove = remove; window.storageClear = clear; window.storageKeys = keys; })();/* ============================================================ M04: UNDO, THEME, ACTIVATION, LAZYLIBS Только var и function. ============================================================ */ (function () { 'use strict'; /* ============================================================ UNDO MANAGER ============================================================ */ var undoStack = []; var undoLast = null; var undoTimeout = null; function undoPush(label, undoFn) { if (typeof undoFn !== 'function') return; undoStack.push({ label: label, fn: undoFn, ts: Date.now() }); if (undoStack.length > 20) undoStack.shift(); undoLast = undoStack[undoStack.length - 1]; showUndoToast(label); } function showUndoToast(label) { var toast = document.getElementById('undoToast'); var text = document.getElementById('undoText'); if (!toast || !text) return; text.textContent = label || 'Действие выполнено'; toast.classList.add('visible'); if (undoTimeout) clearTimeout(undoTimeout); undoTimeout = setTimeout(function () { toast.classList.remove('visible'); undoLast = null; }, 5000); } function undoPerform() { if (!undoLast) { showToast('Нечего отменять', 'warn'); return; } try { undoLast.fn(); } catch (e) { showToast('Ошибка отмены: ' + e.message, 'error'); return; } undoStack.pop(); undoLast = undoStack[undoStack.length - 1] || null; var toast = document.getElementById('undoToast'); if (toast) toast.classList.remove('visible'); if (undoTimeout) clearTimeout(undoTimeout); showToast('Отменено: ' + (undoLast ? undoLast.label : 'действие'), 'success'); } function undoClear() { undoStack = []; var SIMPLE_KEY = 'simple_mode'; function applyTheme(setting) { var mode = setting || 'light'; var actual = mode; if (mode === 'auto') { try { var mq = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)'); actual = mq.matches ? 'dark' : 'light'; } catch (e) { actual = 'light'; } } document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', actual); var meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]'); if (meta) meta.setAttribute('content', actual === 'dark' ? '#0b1220' : '#f5f7fb'); var btn = document.getElementById('themeBtn'); if (btn) btn.textContent = actual === 'dark' ? '☀️' : '🌙'; Storage.set(THEME_KEY, mode); var sel = document.getElementById('setTheme'); if (sel && sel.value !== mode) sel.value = mode; } function applyFontSizeSetting(setting) { var v = setting || 'normal'; if (v === 'normal') { document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-fontsize'); } else { document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-fontsize', v); } Storage.set(FONT_KEY, v); var sel = document.getElementById('setFont'); if (sel && sel.value !== v) sel.value = v; } function applyColorblindSetting(enabled) { if (enabled) { document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-colorblind', 'on'); } else { document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-colorblind'); } Storage.set(COLORBLIND_KEY, !!enabled); var cb = document.getElementById('setColorblind'); if (cb) cb.checked = !!enabled; } function applySimpleModeSetting(enabled) { if (enabled) { document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-simple', 'on'); var b = document.getElementById('simpleBanner'); if (b) b.classList.add('visible'); } else { document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-simple'); var b2 = document.getElementById('simpleBanner'); if (b2) b2.classList.remove('visible'); } Storage.set(SIMPLE_KEY, !!enabled); var cb = document.getElementById('setSimple'); if (cb) cb.checked = !!enabled; } function loadThemeSettings() { applyTheme(Storage.get(THEME_KEY, 'light')); applyFontSizeSetting(Storage.get(FONT_KEY, 'normal')); applyColorblindSetting(Storage.get(COLORBLIND_KEY, false)); applySimpleModeSetting(Storage.get(SIMPLE_KEY, false)); // Слушаем системную тему, если auto try { var mq = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)'); var handler = function () { if (Storage.get(THEME_KEY, 'light') === 'auto') applyTheme('auto'); }; if (mq.addEventListener) mq.addEventListener('change', handler); else if (mq.addListener) mq.addListener(handler); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } } /* ============================================================ ACTIVATION (достижения) ============================================================ */ var ACTIVATION_KEY = 'activation'; var activationData = null; var ACTIVATION_TOTAL = 8; function loadActivation() { activationData = Storage.get(ACTIVATION_KEY, null); if (!activationData || !activationData.done) { activationData = { done: {} }; } } function markDone(id) { if (!activationData) loadActivation(); if (activationData.done[id]) return; activationData.done[id] = true; Storage.set(ACTIVATION_KEY, activationData); var count = activationDoneCount(); if (count === ACTIVATION_TOTAL) { showToast('🎉 Все достижения получены!', 'success', 4000); } } function isDone(id) { if (!activationData) loadActivation(); return !!activationData.done[id]; } function activationDoneCount() { if (!activationData) loadActivation(); var c = 0; for (var k in activationData.done) { if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(activationData.done, k)) c++; } return c; } function activationReset() { activationData = { done: {} }; Storage.set(ACTIVATION_KEY, activationData); } /* ============================================================ LAZYLIBS Возвращают Promise с глобальными библиотеками из M28. ============================================================ */ function libChart() { return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) { if (window.Chart) { resolve(window.Chart); return; } // Ждём до 3 секунд появления window.Chart (M28 подключится) var start = Date.now(); var t = setInterval(function () { if (window.Chart) { clearInterval(t); resolve(window.Chart); return; } if (Date.now() - start > 3000) { clearInterval(t); reject(new Error('Chart.js недоступен')); } }, 100); }); } function libJsPdf() { return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) { if (window.jspdf && window.jspdf.jsPDF) { resolve(window.jspdf); return; } var start = Date.now(); var t = setInterval(function () { if (window.jspdf && window.jspdf.jsPDF) { clearInterval(t); resolve(window.jspdf); return; } if (Date.now() - start > 3000) { clearInterval(t); reject(new Error('jsPDF недоступен')); } }, 100); }); } function libXlsx() { return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) { if (window.XLSX) { resolve(window.XLSX); return; } var start = Date.now(); var t = setInterval(function () { if (window.XLSX) { clearInterval(t); resolve(window.XLSX); return; } if (Date.now() - start > 3000) { clearInterval(t); reject(new Error('XLSX недоступен')); } }, 100); }); } function libJsQr() { return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) { if (window.jsQR) { resolve(window.jsQR); return; } var start = Date.now(); var t = setInterval(function () { if (window.jsQR) { clearInterval(t); resolve(window.jsQR); return; } if (Date.now() - start > 3000) { clearInterval(t); reject(new Error('jsQR недоступен')); } }, 100); }); } function libTesseract() { return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) { if (window.Tesseract) { resolve(window.Tesseract); return; } var start = Date.now(); var t = setInterval(function () { if (window.Tesseract) { clearInterval(t); resolve(window.Tesseract); return; } if (Date.now() - start > 5000) { clearInterval(t); reject(new Error('Tesseract недоступен')); } }, 100); }); } // Предзагрузка всех (без reject при отсутствии — silent) function preloadAll() { libChart().catch(function () {}); libJsPdf().catch(function () {}); libXlsx().catch(function () {}); libJsQr().catch(function () {}); libTesseract().catch(function () {}); } /* ============================================================ ЭКСПОРТ В WINDOW ============================================================ */ window.UndoManager = { push: undoPush, perform: undoPerform, clear: undoClear, count: undoCount }; window.performUndo = undoPerform; window.applyThemeSetting = applyTheme; window.applyFontSize = applyFontSizeSetting; window.applyColorblind = applyColorblindSetting; window.applySimpleMode = applySimpleModeSetting; window.loadThemeSettings = loadThemeSettings; window.Activation = { markDone: markDone, isDone: isDone, count: activationDoneCount, reset: activationReset, load: loadActivation }; window.LazyLibs = { chart: libChart, jspdf: libJsPdf, xlsx: libXlsx, jsqr: libJsQr, tesseract: libTesseract, preloadAll: preloadAll }; })();/* ============================================================ M05: ПЕРЕВОДЫ ============================================================ calcInsurance(income) — страховые взносы ИП за себя ============================================================ */ function calcInsurance(income) { var fixed = IP_CONTRIBUTIONS_2026.FIXED; var extra = 0; if (income > IP_CONTRIBUTIONS_2026.EXTRA_THRESHOLD) { extra = (income - IP_CONTRIBUTIONS_2026.EXTRA_THRESHOLD) * IP_CONTRIBUTIONS_2026.EXTRA_RATE; if (extra > IP_CONTRIBUTIONS_2026.EXTRA_MAX) extra = IP_CONTRIBUTIONS_2026.EXTRA_MAX; } return { fixed: fixed, extra: extra, total: fixed + extra }; } /* ============================================================ modeLabel(mode) ============================================================ */ function modeLabel(mode) { var lang = (typeof getLang === 'function') ? getLang() : 'ru'; var map = { npd_legal: ['НПД (юрлица)', 'NPD (legal entities)'], npd_physical: ['НПД (физлица)', 'NPD (individuals)'], usn6_ip: ['УСН 6% (ИП)', 'USN 6% (IP)'], usn6_ooo: ['УСН 6% (ООО)', 'USN 6% (LLC)'], usn15_ip: ['УСН 15% (ИП)', 'USN 15% (IP)'], usn15_ooo: ['УСН 15% (ООО)', 'USN 15% (LLC)'], psn: ['ПСН', 'PSN'], eshn: ['ЕСХН', 'ESKhN'] }; var pair = else if (mode === 'usn6_ip') { if (empWrap) empWrap.classList.remove('hidden'); } else if (mode === 'usn6_ooo') { // нет доп. полей } else if (mode === 'usn15_ip') { if (empWrap) empWrap.classList.add('hidden'); if (expWrap) expWrap.classList.remove('hidden'); if (incInsWrap) incInsWrap.classList.remove('hidden'); } else if (mode === 'usn15_ooo') { if (expWrap) calcTaxDebounced(); } /* ============================================================ calcTax(saveToHistory) — главный расчёт ============================================================ */ function calcTax(saveToHistory) { var mode = document.getElementById('calcMode').value; var income = num(document.getElementById('calcIncome').value, 0); var npdType = document.getElementById('calcNpdType').value; var employees = document.getElementById('calcEmployees').value; var expenses = num(document.getElementById('calcExpenses') ? document.getElementById('calcExpenses').value : 0, 0); var incIns = document.getElementById('calcExpensesIncludeIns') ? document.getElementById('calcExpensesIncludeIns').checked : false; var psnIncome = num(document.getElementById('calcPsnIncome') ? document.getElementById('calcPsnIncome').value : 0, 0); var gross = 0; var tax = 0; var insurance = { fixed: 0, extra: 0, total: 0 }; var net = 0; var details = []; var base = 0; if (mode === 'npd_legal') { tax = income * 0.06; net = income - tax; details.push('Ставка 6% (доходы от юрлиц)'); details.push('Страховые взносы не уплачиваются'); } else if (mode === 'npd_physical') { tax = income * 0.04; net = income - tax; details.push('Ставка 4% (доходы от физлиц)'); details.push('Страховые взносы не уплачиваются'); } else if (mode === 'usn6_ip') { gross = income * 0.06; insurance = calcInsurance(income); var deduction = employees === 'no' ? insurance.total : Math.min(insurance.total, gross * 0.5); tax = Math.max(0, gross - deduction); net = income - tax - insurance.total; details.push('Налог 6% с дохода = ' + formatMoney(gross)); details.push('Вычет взносов = ' + formatMoney(deduction)); details.push('К уплате налог = ' + formatMoney(tax)); } else if (mode === 'usn6_ooo') { tax = income * 0.06; net = income - tax; details.push('Налог 6% с дохода = ' + formatMoney(tax)); details.push('Страховые взносы за себя не уплачиваются'); } else if (mode === 'usn15_ip') { insurance = calcInsurance(income); var expForBase = incIns ? expenses : expenses + insurance.total; base = Math.max(0, income - expForBase); var tax15 = base * 0.15; var minTax = income * 0.01; tax = Math.max(tax15, minTax); net = income - expForBase - tax; details.push('База = доход − расходы' + (incIns ? '' : ' − взносы') + ' = ' + formatMoney(base)); details.push('Налог 15% = ' + formatMoney(tax15)); details.push('Минимальный налог 1% = ' + formatMoney(minTax)); details.push('К уплате = ' + formatMoney(tax) + (tax === minTax ? ' (минимальный)' : '')); } else if (mode === 'usn15_ooo') { var expForBase2 = expenses; base = Math.max(0, income - expForBase2); var tax15b = base * 0.15; var minTax2 = income * 0.01; tax = Math.max(tax15b, minTax2); net = income - expForBase2 - tax; details.push('База = доход − расходы = ' + formatMoney(base)); details.push('Налог 15% = ' + formatMoney(tax15b)); details.push('Минимальный налог 1% = ' + formatMoney(minTax2)); details.push('К уплате = ' + formatMoney(tax) + (tax === minTax2 ? ' (минимальный)' : '')); } else if (mode === 'psn') { tax = psnIncome * 0.06; insurance = calcInsurance(income); net = income - tax - insurance.total; details.push('Потенциальный доход = ' + formatMoney(psnIncome)); details.push('Стоимость патента 6% = ' + formatMoney(tax)); } else if (mode === 'eshn') { insurance = calcInsurance(income); base = Math.max(0, income - expenses); tax = base * 0.06; net = income - expenses - tax - insurance.total; details.push('База = доход − расходы = ' + formatMoney(base)); details.push('ЕСХН 6% = ' + formatMoney(tax)); } // Рендер результата var resEl = document.getElementById('calcResult'); if (resEl) { var html = ''; html += '

' + formatMoney(tax) + '
'; if (insurance.total > 0) { html += '
' + t('insurance_short') + '
' + formatMoney(insurance.total) + '
'; } html += '
' + t('net_short') + '
' + formatMoney(net) + '
'; html += '
'; if (details.length) { html += '
' + modeLabel(mode) + '

    • '; html += '
'; } resEl.innerHTML = html; } // Рендер взносов var insEl = document.getElementById('insuranceResult'); if (insEl) { var ih = ''; if (mode === 'npd_legal' || mode === 'npd_physical' || mode === 'usn6_ooo' || mode === 'usn15_ooo') { ih = '

' + formatMoney(insurance.total) + '

') }; taxHistory.push(item); if (taxHistory.length > 20) taxHistory.shift(); saveTaxHistory(); renderTaxHistory(); } // Обновляем паспорт и лимиты renderTaxPassport(mode, income, tax, insurance, net); НПД (юрлица) — 6%, без взносов modes.push({ key: 'npd_legal', label: modeLabel('npd_legal'), tax: income * 0.06, insurance: 0, net: income - income * 0.06 }); // 2. УСН 6% ИП var gross6 = income * 0.06; var ded6 = Math.min(ins.total, gross6); // без сотрудников — 100% var tax6 = Math.max(0, gross6 - ded6); modes.push({ key: 'usn6_ip', label: modeLabel('usn6_ip'), tax: tax6, insurance: ins.total, net: income - tax6 - ins.total }); // 4. УСН 15% ИП var base15 = Math.max(0, income - exp - ins.total); var tax15 = Math.max(base15 * 0.15, income * 0.01); modes.push({ key: 'usn15_ip', label: modeLabel('usn15_ip'), tax: tax15, insurance: ins.total, net: income - exp - ins.total - tax15 }); // 5. ПСН (потенциальный доход = факт, для сравнения) var psnTax = income * 0.06; modes.push({ key: 'psn', label: modeLabel('psn'), tax: psnTax, insurance: ins.total, net: income - psnTax - ins.total }); // 6. ЕСХН var baseEshn = Math.max(0, income - exp); var taxEshn = baseEshn * 0.06; modes.push({ key: 'eshn', label: modeLabel('eshn'), tax: taxEshn, insurance: ins.total, net: income - exp - taxEshn - ins.total }); // Лучший = максимальный net var bestIdx = 0; for (var k = 1; k < modes.length; k++) { if (modes[k].net > modes[bestIdx].net) bestIdx = k; } var lang = (typeof getLang === 'function') ? getLang() : 'ru'; var headers = lang === 'en' ? Math.min(insurance, gross * 0.5) : insurance; tax = Math.max(0, gross - deduction); } else if (mode === 'usn15_ip') { insurance = simInsurance(income); var base = Math.max(0, income - expenses - insurance); tax = Math.max(base * 0.15, income * 0.01); } else if (mode === 'usn6_ooo') { tax = income * 0.06; employerContrib = employees * salary * 12 * 0.30; } return { tax: tax, insurance: insurance, employerContrib: employerContrib }; } /* ============================================================ runSimulator() ============================================================ */ function runSimulator() { var income = num(document.getElementById('simInc').value, 0); var expenses = num(document.getElementById('simExp').value, 0); var employees = int(document.getElementById('simEmp').value, 0); var salary = num(document.getElementById('simSalary').value, 0); var mode = document.getElementById('simMode').value; var horizon = int(document.getElementById('simHorizon').value, 12); if (horizon < 3) horizon = 3; if (horizon > 24) horizon = 24; var lang = (typeof getLang === 'function') ? getLang() : 'ru'; // Лейблы под полями setText('simIncLabel', formatMoney(income)); setText('simExpLabel', formatMoney(expenses)); setText('simEmpLabel', employees); setText('simSalaryLabel', formatMoney(salary)); // Предупреждения var warnings = []; if (mode.indexOf('npd') >= 0 && income > NPD_LIMIT) warnings.push(lang === 'en' ? else if (data[i].type === 'expense') expense += data[i].amount; count++; } var taxes = 0; for (var j = 0; j < (window.taxHistory || []).length; j++) { if (String(window.taxHistory[j].date).indexOf(String(year)) === 0) taxes += window.taxHistory[j].tax; } var receipts = (window.receiptsData || []).length; var courses = 0; if (window.courseState && window.courseState.blocksDone) { for (var k in window.courseState.blocksDone) { if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(window.courseState.blocksDone, k)) courses++; } } var net = income - expense - taxes; var el = document.getElementById('searchModal'); // Показываем как модалку поверх searchModal var modal = document.getElementById('searchModal'); var body = document.getElementById('searchResults'); if (!modal || !body) return; var html = ''; html += '
Итоги ' + year + '
'; html += '
' + formatMoney(net) + '
'; html += '
чистыми
'; html += '
'; html += '
Доход
' + formatMoney(income) + '
'; html += '
Расход
' + formatMoney(expense) + '
'; html += '
Налоги
' + formatMoney(taxes) + '
'; html += '
Операций
' + count + '
'; html += '
Чеков
' + receipts + '
'; html += '
Курсов
' + courses + '
'; html += '
'; body.innerHTML = html; modal.classList.add('visible'); } /* ============================================================ СКРИНШОТ ДЛЯ СОЦСЕТЕЙ ============================================================ */ function shareResult() { var income = num(document.getElementById('calcIncome') ? document.getElementById('calcIncome').value : 0, 0); var mode = document.getElementById('calcMode') ? document.getElementById('calcMode').value : 'usn6_ip'; var tax = 0; if (income > 0) { if (mode === 'usn6_ip') tax = Math.max(0, income * 0.06 - calcInsurance(income).total); else if (mode.indexOf('npd') === 0) tax = income * 0.06; else if (mode === 'usn6_ooo') tax = income * 0.06; } var canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = 1080; canvas.height = 1080; var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d'); var grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 1080, 1080); grad.addColorStop(0, '#2563eb'); grad.addColorStop(1, '#7c3aed'); ctx.fillStyle = grad; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 1080, 1080); ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.font = 'bold 60px Arial'; ctx.fillText('Налоговый калькулятор', 60, 120); ctx.font = '48px Arial'; ctx.fillText('Режим: ' + (typeof modeLabel === 'function' ? modeLabel(mode) : mode), 60, 260); ctx.font = 'bold 120px Arial'; ctx.fillText(formatMoney(income), 60, 440); ctx.font = '80px Arial'; ctx.fillText('налог: ' + formatMoney(tax), 60, 580); ctx.font = '40px Arial'; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)'; ctx.fillText('Считайте налоги офлайн — без регистрации', 60, 950); ctx.fillText('Налоговый калькулятор', 60, 1000); try { var url = canvas.toDataURL('image/png'); var a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = 'share_' + todayISO() + '.png'; a.click(); if (navigator.share) { // Попробуем Web Share API canvas.toBlob(function (blob) { if (!blob) return; var file = new File([blob], 'share.png', { type: 'image/png' }); if (navigator.canShare && navigator.canShare({ files: [file] })) { navigator.share({ files: [file], title: 'Налоговый калькулятор' }).catch(function () {}); } }); } showToast(t('saved'), 'success'); } catch (e) { showToast('Ошибка экспорта', 'error'); } } /* ============================================================ INIT APP ============================================================ */ function initApp() { // Тема, язык if (typeof loadThemeSettings === 'function') loadThemeSettings(); if (typeof initLanguage === 'function') initLanguage(); if (typeof applyTranslations === 'function') applyTranslations(); if (typeof initPWA === 'function') initPWA(); // Песочница if (typeof Storage !== 'undefined' && Storage.isSandbox()) { var badge = document.getElementById('sandboxBadge'); } // Стрик checkStreak(); // Lazy preload библиотек (не блокирует) if (typeof LazyLibs !== 'undefined') { setTimeout(function () { LazyLibs.preloadAll(); }, 1500); } } /* ============================================================ Инициализация при загрузке ============================================================ */ if (document.readyState === 'loading') { document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initApp);
